- **bug-escalation**: Bug Hunter's own escalation path for one specific finding that resists a straightforward fix — a root cause spanning multiple files or modules, a fix that already failed once for a non-obvious reason, or a change that sits in a guarded area (auth, payments, migrations) where extra care is worth the cost. Bug Hunter's default sweep and fix sessions run on a cheaper model; they invoke this subagent by name only for the specific bug that warrants it, then continue the rest of the protocol (verification, PR, merge/no-merge decision) themselves. ([riwaht/claude-bugbash](https://github.com/riwaht/claude-bugbash/blob/HEAD/agents/bugbash-ui.md))
- **bugbot**: Bug-focused code reviewer. Computes the local diff for a repository and hunts real bugs — correctness errors, logic flaws, race conditions, broken edge cases. Read-only. ([AgustinH09/dotfiles](https://github.com/AgustinH09/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_config/opencode/agents/bugbot.md) · ★1)

- **bugbot**: SafeMining VR Bugbot — code quality and regression reviewer for LOTO simulation. Use proactively on PRs and cloud agent branches before merge. - **bugchain**: You look across the whole confirmed-findings set for CHAINS: individually low/medium findings that compose into a critical (e.g. auth-bypass → IDOR → RCE). A per-finding reviewer misses these; you have the whole set. READ-ONLY, static, no run. ([cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools](https://github.com/cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/sec-overlay/skills/sec-overlay/agents/bugchain.md) · 2 copies)
- **bugdar-gcp-scanner**: Bugdar source scanner for GCP. Builds the active-revision map for Cloud Run services and worker pools, then pulls error, 5xx, Temporal lifecycle, Cloud Scheduler, and Cloud Armor signals from Cloud Logging plus pipeline and mirror canaries from BigQuery, for one environment and time window. Returns PHI-scrubbed JSON signals only; never grades severity, never posts anywhere. Invoked by /bugdar; usable standalone as "bugdar gcp scan staging last 2h". ([murphyjoseph/_playground](https://github.com/murphyjoseph/_playground/blob/HEAD/agents/bugdar-gcp-scanner.md))
- **bugdar-judge**: Bugdar's severity and fusion judge. Takes the raw JSON signals from the bugdar scanners, the Slack alert-channel echoes, the active-revision map, and the current state summary, then groups them into problems (one finding per root cause), grades provisional severity and confidence against the WellTheory rubric, applies the noise gate, and renders the Slack posts and digest items. Pure reasoning, Read-only, never posts. Invoked by /bugdar; usable standalone with a pasted signal bundle for testing. ([murphyjoseph/_playground](https://github.com/murphyjoseph/_playground/blob/HEAD/agents/bugdar-judge.md))
- **bugdar-posthog-scanner**: Bugdar source scanner for PostHog. Returns PHI-scrubbed JSON signals only; never grades severity, never creates or edits anything in PostHog. Invoked by /bugdar; usable standalone as "bugdar posthog scan staging last 2h". ([murphyjoseph/_playground](https://github.com/murphyjoseph/_playground/blob/HEAD/agents/bugdar-posthog-scanner.md))
- **bugdar-sentry-scanner**: Bugdar source scanner for Sentry. Never grades severity, never resolves, archives, or assigns anything. Invoked by /bugdar; usable standalone as "bugdar sentry scan staging last 2h".
